# Task Progress & Milestone Ledger (TASK_PROGRESS.md)
**Project**: Shree Ashirwad Packers and Movers (`shreeashirwadpackers`)  
**Scope**: Historical milestone log for long-running and multi-phase tasks.

---

## 1. Operating Protocol
1. **Milestones Only**: Do not log individual file views, queries, or micro-edits here. Only log significant phase completions, architectural sign-offs, or verified major milestones.
2. **Append-Only History**: Preserve previous milestone entries to provide an immutable trail of accomplished work across extended sessions.
3. **Context Anchor**: If a conversation experiences context compaction or reset, use the last completed milestone entry as the verified starting point.

---

## 2. Active Milestone Track
- **Current Track**: System Initialization
- **Active Phase**: Baseline Established
- **Overall Status**: Complete

### Milestone 9: Resolved Duplicate Headers & nav.php Include Warning
- **Timestamp**: 2026-09-30 12:20 IST
- **Phase Objective**: Fix the PHP stream error in `bike-transport-in-dumka.php` and eliminate the duplicate header rendering in `car-transport-in-dumka.php` and other Dumka cluster pages.
- **Root Cause Identified**:
  1. Image 1 Warning: `bike-transport-in-dumka.php` attempted to include non-existent `includes/nav.php` inside `<body>`.
  2. Image 2 Double Header: `header.php` outputs visual HTML markup (`topbar` and `site-header`). Calling `require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/header.php'` at the very top of each PHP file before `<!DOCTYPE html>`, combined with `include __DIR__ . '/../includes/header.php'` inside `<body>`, resulted in the header rendering twice on the page.
- **Key Deliverables**:
  - Replaced top-level `header.php` require with `require_once __DIR__ . '/includes/config.php'; require_once __DIR__ . '/includes/seo.php';` across all 7 Dumka pages.
  - Replaced `nav.php` with `<?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/header.php'; ?>` inside `<body>` of `bike-transport-in-dumka.php`.
  - Expanded content in `dumka-to-ranchi-packers-and-movers.php` to 2,161 words (maintaining >2,000 words after duplicate header removal).
- **Verification Result**:
  - Live server test executed across all 8 Dumka cluster URLs: 0 warnings, exactly 1 `siteHeader` and 1 `topbar` per page, all pages confirmed >2,000 visible words, 100% schemas valid, HTTP 200 OK.
- **Status**: COMPLETED

Additionally, two-column `<aside class="sidebar-wrapper">` widgets caused layout squishing and horizontal breaking on mobile screens.

  - `scratch/audit_phusro_20.py` confirmed 20/20 PASSED with 100% header/footer brand integrity.

### Milestone 30: Sitewide Mobile Viewport Overflow & Horizontal Cutoff Resolution
- **Timestamp**: 2026-10-17 17:45 IST
- **Phase Objective**: Diagnose and comprehensively resolve horizontal overflow and side cutting reported on mobile devices (e.g., `kolkata-to-phusro-packers-and-movers` in 382px DevTools viewport), audit other page architectures (`ranchi-to-deoghar`, `ranchi-to-dumka`, `packers-and-movers-in-dhanbad`), and establish universal responsive guards.
- **Root Causes Identified**:
  1. Route Hero Grid (`.route-hero .container`): 2-column desktop grid (`1.2fr 0.8fr`) lacked responsive stacking below 768px, and `.form-row` had `display: flex;` with 2 inputs side-by-side, forcing grid width to 479px on 382px screens.
  2. H1 & Badge Wrapping: Unbroken title strings at 2.5rem/2.1rem and unconstrained badges caused text lines to extend past the right edge and get clipped by `body { overflow-x: hidden }` ("Packers and Movers Kol" with "kata" cut off).
  3. Unconstrained Table Wrappers: Tables inside `.route-main-content`, `.route-dumka-container`, etc., lacked `width: 100% !important; max-width: 100% !important; box-sizing: border-box !important`, causing 550px+ tables to push `<main>` width to 590px–628px.
  4. Decorative Glow Overflow: Absolute positioned `.hero-glow-2` with `right: -100px` without parent overflow containment pushed body scrollWidth on hub pages.
- **Key Deliverables**:
  - `assets/css/style.src.css`, `assets/css/style.css`, and `assets/css/style.min.css`: Added universal mobile viewport guards:
    - Guaranteed `html, body { overflow-x: hidden !important; max-width: 100% !important; width: 100% !important; }`
    - Constrained `.route-hero .container` to `grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) !important;` on mobile with stacked single-column form inputs and full-width CTA buttons.
    - Added responsive font scaling (`1.75rem` / `1.55rem`) with `overflow-wrap: break-word !important` on `.hero-content h1`.
    - Protected all table wrappers (`[class*="table-"][class*="-wrapper"]`, `.table-responsive`, etc.) with `display: block !important; width: 100% !important; max-width: 100% !important; overflow-x: auto !important; -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch !important;`.
    - Contained all decorative glow elements (`.hero-glow-2`, etc.) and parent sections with `overflow: hidden !important`.
- **Verification Result**:
  - Headless Chromium CDP benchmark tested 20+ diverse pages across all corridors at 382x642: **20/20 PASSED** with `bodyScrollWidth = 382` (zero horizontal overflow, zero cutting off).
  - Screenshots captured and verified visually for `kolkata-to-phusro`, `ranchi-to-deoghar`, and `packers-and-movers-in-dhanbad`.
- **Status**: COMPLETED


### Milestone 31: Sitewide Component CSS & Unstyled Elements Resolution
- **Timestamp**: 2026-10-17 18:15 IST
- **Phase Objective**: Resolve sitewide unstyled components (breadcrumbs rendering as raw numbered list, quote form button as unstyled browser input, FAQ accordions displaying all open text boxes, unstyled bullet lists, vehicle and corporate mobility cards) across Ormanjhi routes and all template archetypes without modifying global `:root` variables or header/footer styles.
- **Root Cause Diagnosed**:
  - `pages/ormanjhi-to-hazaribagh-packers-and-movers.php` (and other earlier corridor pages/templates) relied on component classes (`.breadcrumb-container`, `.breadcrumb`, `.breadcrumb-item`, `.quote-card`, `.route-lead-form`, `.btn-submit-lead`, `.sub-badge`, `.route-specs-list`, `.tariff-table`, `.layer-item`, `.vehicle-card`, `.card-features-list`, `.doc-item`, `.locality-tags`, `.process-grid`, `.process-card`, `.checklist-item`, `.faq-card`, `.collapse`) that were missing from `assets/css/style.css`.
  - 182 pages using `.breadcrumb-item` had no default list reset or separator styles, falling back to raw browser `<ol>` numbers.
  - The FAQ accordion lacked `.collapse { display: none; }` and `.collapse.show { display: block; }`, causing all answers to display at once in unstyled rectangular boxes.
- **Key Deliverables**:
  - `assets/css/style.src.css`, `assets/css/style.css`, `assets/css/style.min.css`: Added rich, modular component CSS for Breadcrumbs, Hero Quote Form, Route Profile, Tariff Tables, Packing 5-Layers, Vehicle Transit cards, Corporate Relocation perks, Localities & Tags, 5-Step Process cards, Pre-Move Checklist cards, FAQ Accordions, and CTA banners.
  - `assets/js/main.js`: Added Bootstrap-compatible accordion toggle event listener for `[data-toggle="collapse"]` with smooth active state management.
- **Verification Results**:
  - Headless Chromium CDP desktop (1200px) and mobile (382px) renders verified with live screenshots:
    - `scratch/after_fix_ormanjhi_top.png`: Breadcrumbs rendered as clean horizontal bar with icons and `/` separator; quote submit button styled with vibrant orange gradient and drop shadow; input fields cleanly carded with focus borders.
    - `scratch/after_fix_ormanjhi_full.png`: All sections (Logistics Corridor, Tariff Table, 5-Layer Protocol, Vehicle Cards, Corporate Relocations, Localities, 5-Step Workflow, Checklist, and FAQ Accordions) fully styled and visually balanced.
    - `scratch/after_fix_ormanjhi_mobile_top.png`: Mobile view 100% responsive with zero horizontal overflow (`bodyScrollWidth = 382`).
  - 10 diverse pages benchmarked across desktop and mobile: 10/10 PASSED.
- **Status**: COMPLETED

    - **201 Keywords (47.5%)**: Verified as already fully covered by live high-authority pages, district hubs, and utility tools.
